FLATTENER THIRD-PARTY NOTICES
Flattener original source is MIT-licensed. Third-party software, fonts, character maps, color profiles and OCR models retain their own licenses. No endorsement by their authors is implied.

===== README.md =====
# OCR models

Flattener embeds Robert Knight's Ocrs text-detection and text-recognition models so recognition works offline from the first launch. These files are unmodified upstream RTen models, not Flattener-authored software. They recognize Latin-script text; output can contain recognition errors and is not a verified transcription.

The model weights are licensed under **Creative Commons Attribution-ShareAlike 4.0 International (CC BY-SA 4.0)**. Flattener's application license does not replace that license. Distributions containing the models must retain this attribution and the accompanying [license text](LICENSE-CC-BY-SA-4.0.txt). Changes to the model weights: none.

Creator: Robert Knight / Ocrs project.

Source: [Ocrs model card](https://huggingface.co/robertknight/ocrs) and [training project](https://github.com/robertknight/ocrs-models).

Pinned upstream revision: `df0edd170279ab971b53e094c627255a87e1a503`.

| File | Bytes | SHA-256 |
|---|---:|---|
| text-detection-ssfbcj81.rten | 2,523,564 | 614aafabf27c94d386f7aa036c967c2e47e4b9938fa11531ca8f5698c1ca4c36 |
| text-rec-checkpoint-s52qdbqt.rten | 9,716,444 | 606d9a0414c6b73c99df75b707c11c70d1c8b12e1d4f900922e185fc37bfca65 |

The downloaded bytes were checked against the upstream repository's SHA-256 LFS object identifiers. Reproducible source URLs:

- [Detection model](https://huggingface.co/robertknight/ocrs/resolve/df0edd170279ab971b53e094c627255a87e1a503/text-detection-ssfbcj81.rten)
- [Recognition model](https://huggingface.co/robertknight/ocrs/resolve/df0edd170279ab971b53e094c627255a87e1a503/text-rec-checkpoint-s52qdbqt.rten)

The Ocrs and RTen Rust software libraries have their own MIT/Apache-2.0 licenses. The model weights retain CC BY-SA 4.0. No affiliation or endorsement by their authors is implied.

The pinned detector uses a **0.5 text-classification threshold**, as [documented by its maintainer](https://github.com/robertknight/ocrs/issues/254#issuecomment-4620351611). Ocrs 0.13.1 defaults to 0.2 for an older detector; Flattener applies the matching threshold through the public probability-mask API. This changes application postprocessing, not the model files or weights. The adapted contour code retains its [MIT notice](../../licenses/Ocrs-NOTICE.md).

===== Hayro-NOTICE.md =====
The following parts of code have been adapted/translated from [PDFBox](https://github.com/apache/pdfbox), available 
under the Apache 2.0 license.
 - The encoding maps for some of the standard fonts.
 - The evaluator for type 0 functions.
 - The list of common dictionary keys.

The following parts of code have been adapted/translated from [pdf.js](https://github.com/mozilla/pdf.js), available
under the Apache 2.0 license:
- The CalRGB and CalGray conversion code.
- The flate decoder.
- The implementation of AES, MD5, SHA and RC4.

The following parts of code have been copied from the [png crate](https://github.com/image-rs/image-png), available under the Apache 2.0:
- Decoding of PNG predictors.

UBUNTU FONT LICENCE Version 1.0
-------------------------------

PREAMBLE
This licence allows the licensed fonts to be used, studied, modified and

bundled, embedded, and redistributed provided the terms of this licence
are met. The fonts and derivatives, however, cannot be released under

licence does not require any document created using the fonts or their

the fonts.

DEFINITIONS
"Font Software" refers to the set of files released by the Copyright
Holder(s) under this licence and clearly marked as such. This may
include source files, build scripts and documentation.

"Original Version" refers to the collection of Font Software components
as received under this licence.

"Modified Version" refers to any derivative made by adding to, deleting,
or substituting -- in part or in whole -- any of the components of the
Original Version, by changing formats or by porting the Font Software to
a new environment.

"Copyright Holder(s)" refers to all individuals and companies who have a
copyright ownership of the Font Software.

"Substantially Changed" refers to Modified Versions which can be easily
identified as dissimilar to the Font Software by users of the Font
Software comparing the Original Version with the Modified Version.

To "Propagate" a work means to do anything with it that, without
permission, would make you directly or secondarily liable for
infringement under applicable copyright law, except executing it on a
computer or modifying a private copy. Propagation includes copying,
distribution (with or without modification and with or without charging

(b) Modified Versions which are Substantially Changed must be renamed to
avoid use of the name of the Original Version or similar names entirely.
(c) Modified Versions which are not Substantially Changed must be
renamed to both (i) retain the name of the Original Version and (ii) add
additional naming elements to distinguish the Modified Version from the
Original Version. The name of such Modified Versions must be the name of
the Original Version, with "derivative X" where X represents the name of
the new work, appended to that name.

3) The name(s) of the Copyright Holder(s) and any contributor to the
Font Software shall not be used to promote, endorse or advertise any
Modified Version, except (i) as required by this licence, (ii) to
acknowledge the contribution(s) of the Copyright Holder(s) or (iii) with

===== LIBRE BASKERVILLE INTERFACE FONT =====

# Libre Baskerville

Flattener uses Libre Baskerville for its interface text, including controls and the license viewer.

Source: [Google Fonts / Libre Baskerville](https://github.com/google/fonts/tree/b3d4b3ba7c4d54f15ed2be72d7f58b9097c3b252/ofl/librebaskerville).

The unmodified variable regular font is stored as `LibreBaskerville.ttf` for a stable asset path. Upstream filename: `LibreBaskerville[wght].ttf`.

Revision: `b3d4b3ba7c4d54f15ed2be72d7f58b9097c3b252`.

SHA-256: `05a95421961341c5b2556285e8415df9db27dab4f4abe22b446b3c6a8b916c5d`.

===== OCRS CONTOUR SOURCE ADAPTATION =====
# Ocrs contour postprocessing

The word-rectangle contour postprocessing in `src/ocr.rs` is adapted from Ocrs 0.13.1, `ocrs/src/detection.rs`, at upstream revision `e5f1c5205326804637368422fe420aa1b9769676`.

Source: https://github.com/robertknight/ocrs/blob/e5f1c5205326804637368422fe420aa1b9769676/ocrs/src/detection.rs

The adaptation uses the public probability-mask API and a 0.5 threshold appropriate to the bundled Hugging Face detector. It retains the upstream polygon simplification, rectangle expansion, and minimum-area settings. The threshold requirement is documented by the maintainer at https://github.com/robertknight/ocrs/issues/254#issuecomment-4620351611.

This source adaptation is used under the upstream MIT license, reproduced below. The separately bundled model weights remain subject to CC BY-SA 4.0.

Copyright (c) 2023 Ocrs project contributors.

the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software
is furnished to do so, subject to the following
conditions:
